Each execution surface must carry raw evidence and an independent\r\nchecker result.\r\n\r\n## AO lane 獨立性與 false-green 陷阱（2026-10-10，預設適用）\r\n\r\n獨立查核 lane 的價值來自「**能被證明**」，不是「看起來獨立」。以下規則為本 repo 通則：\r\n\r\n1. **閘門在 spawn 前 fail-closed**：受點名的 arm 不健康即拒絕（具名錯誤碼、非零 exit、**不 spawn**）；**永不**靜默改跑其他 arm 來讓回合「成功」。\r\n2. **required ＝ served**：`--require-arm` 意指該 arm 必須**實際服務**本回合；只通過閘門不算。要確保用到特定模型，**同時釘 rank**。\r\n3. **升權 lane 必須帶守衛主體**：`--guard-subject` 為條件必備；守衛根**無法解析**時必須拒絕（0 attempts），**不得**回報「乾淨」。受檢主體的 `removed`/`changed` 使該回合 verdict **自我作廢**。\r\n4. **空泛通過（vacuous pass）禁令**：任何比較器／守衛若在**輸入缺失**時回報成功，即為缺陷。以三值判定（`PASS` / `FAIL` / **`INCONCLUSIVE`**）實作，`INCONCLUSIVE` 一律視為**未驗收**；比較前先斷言輸入非空。\r\n5. **自我指涉禁令**：工件**不得**記錄自身的 digest／大小，也**不得**由「正在寫入它的那條指令」同時被 digest。排除要**由構造達成**，不可事後手改 manifest。\r\n6. **負測必須是「本應通過」的回合**：若該回合因其他條件（缺 nonce、缺用量收據、缺 arm）先被拒，則它證明不了受測條件。優先使用**確定性（無模型）**接縫驅動負測。\r\n7. **promotion ≠ acceptance**：promotion 是狀態轉移；acceptance 必須由**獨立** checker lane 產生 verdict，且 oracle 要在檢查**之前**登記。maker 只**機械化轉錄** verdict（fail-closed 映射），**不得**自判；`actor` 欄位必須區分「判決作者」與「動列者」。\r\n8. **不得以「重新登入同一帳號」主張身分分離**：單一帳號環境下，重複的互動登入對身分分離毫無作用；此類目標應記為 **NOT APPLICABLE**，而非 blocked/deferred。\r\n9. **成本與副作用必須實測後如實更正**：預估與實測不符時，以實測值更正紀錄（例：relay 帳本列數），不得沿用較好看的預估。\r\n\r\n## Session plane ≠ lane plane (mandatory, 2026-10-30)\r\n\r\nThe interactive session runs on ONE pinned arm — on this deployment `opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash`,\r\nwhich is the **EXECUTE** arm. A consumer must never issue direct SQL to\r\n  change acceptance state. The canonical transition is performed by the spine / domain owner,\r\n  preserves the canonical event + audit trace, is fail-closed on subject/evidence binding, rejects\r\n  stale or conflicting subjects, is idempotent on identical replay, and is transactional.\r\n- **Ordering decisions use the implicit `rowid` (monotonic insertion order), never wall-clock\r\n  `created_at`.** `created_at` has second granularity, so two rows sharing a timestamp make the\r\n  choice arbitrary. Guarded by `tests/test_ordering_oracle.py`; a wall-clock ordering site in\r\n  `src/hg_kseos` fails that guard.\r\n- Both are engineering-base changes with a recorded rollback path and an independent checker\r\n  result; neither authorizes production, release, or a second control plane.\r\n\r\n## Admission chain (no durable free-form mutation)\r\n\r\n```text\r\ngoal / source → source admission → Requirement (freeze) → TaskSpec → WorkOrder (writer=codex)\r\n```\r\n\r\n- No durable free-form mutation: every normative step is admitted (SharedSpine EVT) and frozen.\r\n- Unadmitted or unfrozen material is candidate data, never normative.\r\n- WorkOrder is the normative task truth; Kanban is coordination state, not authority.\r\n\r\n## Routing (Direct / Kanban / Swarm)\r\n\r\n- Lane selection is deterministic (HGK router), not LLM guesswork and not user choice in the normal\r\n  path: DIRECT for small atomic tasks, Kanban for durable / cross-role / restart-surviving\r\n  (project-scoped), Swarm on demand for independent parallel lanes.\r\n- Tracked implementation goes to Codex (`writer=codex`); route decisions are recorded in the\r\n  WorkOrder / evidence.\r\n- `NOT_SELECTED_WITH_REASON` is a valid router disposition when a named method is not selected;\r\n  silent fallback and fake invocation are prohibited (explicit `DEGRADED` + certified native fallback).\r\n\r\n## Profile / worker binding\r\n\r\n- Discover the Profile → verify its Distribution and effective load → bind the canonical\r\n  ExecutionBinding → dispatch. Unknown or unverified assignee → `BLOCK_BEFORE_ASSIGN`.\r\n- ROLE ≠ PROFILE ≠ WORKER: identity, capability contract, and active worker claim are distinct.\r\n- One writer per worktree; worker liveness = valid claim + recent heartbeat (no ghost workers).\r\n\r\n## Fabric consumption\r\n\r\n- Discover policy → bind exact version/digest (frozen contract sha) → record consumption trace\r\n  (policy artifact + sha256 + consumer + decision + evidence) → apply SoD / permission / risk /\r\n  change / BreakGlass rules.\r\n- Policy presence is never a PASS proxy: a policy file existing is not consumption\r\n  (`yaml_presence_alone: NOT_ACCEPTED`). Follow the F0 trace pattern\r\n  (`..\\Fabric\\stage\\RP002-STAGE-HGK\\F0\\F0_POLICY_CONSUMER_TRACE.json`).\r\n\r\n## Acceptance\r\n\r\n- Maker / writer / orchestrator ≠ final checker: the maker can never self-accept, and the commander\r\n  can never sign the final AcceptanceReceipt.\r\n- Acceptance Officer runs `VERIFY_ONLY`: no candidate write, no repair, no WorkOrder create, no\r\n  promotion / release / deploy (fresh session, read-only candidate, 8 acceptance packs).\r\n- Officer cannot repair the candidate it checks; defects are reported to the maker, then rechecked\r\n  independently.\r\n- **Independent checker lane (advisory binding, candidate)**: `REQ-CHU-04-CHECKER-ADAPTER` +\r\n  `REQ-CHU-04-WIRING`. A dispatched checker (Codex CLI -> loopback bridge -> a model distinct from the\r\n  maker) produces an **advisory verdict only**. It is never an OracleReceipt and never a canonical\r\n  acceptance status; the runtime consumes it through one named method that carries an explicit advisory\r\n  ceiling, and no canonical token may be derived from it. The adapter fails closed on a binding that\r\n  claims a canonical token, a non-candidate binding, a verdict outside the advisory enum, and a verdict\r\n  whose digests disagree with the intake. Watchdog pauses are advisory requests\r\n  (`WATCHDOG_ADVISORY_ONLY`); resuming canonical state always requires an operator decision.\r\n  Landed at `src/hg_kseos/checker_bridge/` with its suite under `tests/checker_bridge/`\r\n  (113 tests, OK, skipped=1). Round summary and ceilings:\r\n  `evidence/checker-upgrade-20261009/IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.md`. Honest status: the cutover gate is\r\n  `TEMP_CLOSED/UNVERIFIED`, NOT accepted/released; `hgk_canonical_acceptance: NOT_PERFORMED`.\r\n  Deferred by the specification's section 9.3 and NOT claimed here: the two User Guide currentness\r\n  edits, which happen only after cutover is formally accepted.\r\n\r\n## Knowledge\r\n\r\n- Canonical source / approved ≠ RAG / KG / wiki: Obsidian, LLM Wiki, RAG, KG, and agent notes are\r\n  derived or candidate surfaces.\r\n- Agents write candidates only (`hgk.candidate.*`); promotion is owner/gate only\r\n  (no agent self-promote); revoked / stale namespaces are excluded; forget = tombstone + audit\r\n  receipt. Follow `..\\Fabric\\stage\\RP002-STAGE-HGK\\KG1\\*` evidence.\r\n\r\n## Governed evolution\r\n\r\n- Evidence-backed gaps only: gap → FIT-GAP → candidate → sandbox → eval (incl. negative/security/\r\n  holdout) → independent verification → promotion → canary → rollback.\r\n- Promotion requires a Human Policy Owner gate (COV-11-06); `UNBOUNDED_SELF_MODIFICATION=0`.\r\n\r\n## Evidence / claims\r\n\r\n- PASS requires raw evidence with subject/candidate binding (master/stage/subject digests); stale or\r\n  foreign evidence is invalid; summary-only evidence is invalid.\r\n- Critical writes are read back and hashed; checkpoint / resume / idempotency keys are used for\r\n  replay-safe execution (`worker_exit_zero_is_pass: false`).\r\n- Blocking TT/CR carry forward; never silently dropped.\r\n\r\n## Stage boundary\r\n\r\n- RP-002 Stage-2 is externally accepted (`..\\Fabric\\stage\\RP002-STAGE-HGK\\EXTERNAL_ACCEPTANCE_RECEIPT_STAGE2.yaml`).\r\n- Stage-3 entry is NOT authorized until the Stage-3 contract is compiled and SQP1 is authorized;\r\n  next gate = SQP1. **This docs task must NOT execute SQP1 or Stage-3.**\r\n- Production / live / remote: NOT_CLAIMED; `sqs_live_trading: NOT_AUTHORIZED`.\r\n[\n  'agents',\n  'AGENTS.md',\n  'docs',\n  'knowledge',\n  'prompts',\n  'release',\n  'runtime',\n  'skills',\n  'state-machines',\n  'tooling',\n  'workflows'\n]"}],"_meta":{"codex/nodeReplExecutionDurationMs":1},"structured_content":null},"error":null,"status":"completed"}}

自己跑，不是寫檢查的人自己說）\n\n`tools/acceptance_rerun.py`：**每個檢查都是新行程**，輸出落在 `evidence/ACCEPTANCE_RERUN/`，逐項比對期望 exit。\n\n```\n14 checks / 0 failed\nL1.selftest P0 · L1.r25-scan P0 · L2.selftest P0 · L2.menu-epoch41 P0 · L2.retired-arm P9\nL3.gate-payload P0 · L3.ed25519 P0 · L3.ed25519-selftest P0 · L4.validate-pos P0 · L4.pytest P0\nL6.pytest P0 · L6.validate P0 · L5.selftest P0 · L5.pytest P0\n```\n\n另外三項由我本人直接執行、不經任何車道腳本：\n\n1. **G24 負例**：把 `l1.bytes` 改回 `111331` 的 payload 鏡像 → `VERDICT FAIL pass=13/16 blocking_fail=2`，首個 FAIL 為 `G24 SOURCE_CONTRACT_BYTES_MISMATCH`。\n2. **七份簽章**：`7/7 signatures verified against owner key d42c654cd1afba80` + `FINDING_R26-06_TZ_UNQUALIFIED`。\n3. **r27 append-only**：`prefix identical: True`，r27 688,148 B vs r26 667,205 B（payload LF 分身）。\n\n### 5.1 獨立 VERIFY_SECURITY 車道的裁決，以及它抓到我的兩個錯\n\n`R27VERIFYACCEPT`（`openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol`，獨立 Codex CLI 行程、read-only，306.37 s，usage 1,194,662 in / 10,542 out）回的是 **5 CONFIRMED / 12 UNPROVEN / 0 REFUTED**，全文在 `out/VERIFY_LANE_VERDICT.md`。\n\n**它確認的**：C9（L1 `6224679` B / `28005` lines / `cbdd75fd…`）、C10（19 clauses → **76** properties，逐 WP 給出 32/20/48/16/8/4）、C11（`full.excluded={}`，六項在 `not_runnable_in_CI`）、C12（r27 prefix 逐位元等於 r26；667,205 B / `09c666da…` vs 688,148 B / `151645f8…`）、C13（`merge-base` 空輸出 exit 1、`90 / 328`）。另外它用 Node 的 crypto **獨立驗到 7/7 簽章**，並自行重做了「反轉一個 byte 必失敗」的變異。\n\n**它沒能驗的 12 項，原因不是修補可疑，而是三個環境／設計問題，其中兩個是我的錯**：\n\n1. **`spawn ...Python311/python.exe ENOENT`** — checker 的子行程起不來 Python，所以所有 Python 支撐的檢查（C1–C4、C7、C15–C17）根本沒開始，`exit` 是 `ENOENT` 而不是應用程式的退出碼。它誠實寫 `UNPROVEN` 而非 `REFUTED`。\n2. **我的 brief 自相矛盾（我的缺陷）** — 我一面要求 read-only，一面要它跑「會寫 scratch fixture」的自測（`verify_quorum.py:441–452` 寫檔、`selftest_fhs_3lm.py` 會 `shutil.rmtree`）。它依規定拒絕寫入，於是 C5/C6 無法執行。這是**驗收 brief 的設計錯誤**，不是修補的缺陷。\n3. **`fatal: detected dubious ownership`** — git 在它那個子行程身分下拒絕讀 `hgk-bridge`，C14 因此 UNPROVEN。\n4. C17 還暴露我給的複現命令不完整：`--profile payload` 不會選到 payload scope，需要 `--scope payload`（`gate_r20.py:2048/2050/2072`）。命令是我寫錯的，不是 gate 壞了。\n\n**這一節因此說明兩件事**：真正的異模型／異行程驗收**已經發生**且**有收據**；而它最有價值的產出不是「PASS」，是**抓出 maker 的驗收指令本身有三處不可執行**。\n\n---\n\n## 6｜HGK 血緣：本輪真正量到的事實\n\n```\ngit merge-base acd9196f 86f40ec        -> (no output) exit 1\ngit merge-base --is-ancestor 雙向       -> exit 1 / exit 1\nroot(main)   = a2389c138cf51641c95494c3fa551c249a6c0058\nroot(branch) = ec58a0be385b2b387bb7fb336aceef90259e7a1a\ngit rev-list --left-right --count        -> 90    328\nis-shallow: false ; grafts: none ; refs/replace: none\n```\n\n**判決**：`DISJOINT`。`main_is_ancestor=true` 與 `commits_ahead=327` 在本 clone 上**不成立**；兩條歷史並列而非快轉區間。\n**處置**：不 merge、不用 `--allow-unrelated-histories`、不改 default branch；改以 `main` 衍生的內容移植 PR #16（config 面 14 檔分類 `PORT 3 / REPLAY 2 / DO_NOT_MERGE 11`，其餘 2,456 路徑一次性記為 `OUT_OF_SCOPE_FOR_THIS_PR`）。\n\n---\n\n## 7｜仍未關閉（具名，不粉飾）\n\n| 未關項 | 卡在哪 | 誰能關 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 10｜本報告對自身先前版本的更正（append-only，不覆寫）\n\n先前 commit `d4a4fa1` 的本報告寫著 `VERIFY_LONG_TURN_NO_RECEIPT`，理由是兩次長回合都看不到收據。**那個結論是錯的，原因是我讀得太早**：wrapper 那一次（`R27VERIFYACCEPT`）實際在 **306.37 s** 後成功並落地 `receipt.json`（`ok=true`、exit 0、nonce present、usage 1,194,662 in / 10,542 out），只是我檢查目錄時它還沒寫完。\n\n更正如下，舊敘述在 §2 `R26-11`、§3 VERIFY 列與 §5.1 已被取代：\n\n| 舊敘述 | 更正後 |\n|---|---|\n| 長回合無收據 → 只能靠 Commander 自跑 | **長回合 served 並有收據**；獨立異模型驗收成立，其裁決為 5 CONFIRMED / 12 UNPROVEN / 0 REFUTED |\n| `acceptance_independence: PROCESS_AND_EVIDENCE_PATH_ONLY` | `DISTINCT_PROCESS_AND_MODEL_FOR_THE_VERIFY_TURN`（但 child exec surface 限制了它的可達範圍） |\n| `VERIFY_LONG_TURN_NO_RECEIPT` | `SERVED_IN_480S_WINDOW`；前檯 330 s 重跑確實 `TIMEOUT`，那是視窗太短，不是 lane 不服務 |\n\n教訓（寫進本輪自己的紀錄）：**在 detached lane 上「看不到檔案」不等於「沒有產出」；收據是延遲寫入的，宣告 BLOCKED 之前必須先確認行程是否仍活著、或等到 timeout 邊界之後再看一次。** 這正是本輪在修的同一類假綠／假紅問題。\n\n---\n\n## 11｜七個車道自報回來後，必須修正／補記的五件事\n\n車道的最終自報與我先前的紀錄有五處不一致。以下每一條都以磁碟上的檔案重驗過，不是轉述。\n\n| # | 我先前的敘述 | 車道最終自報／重驗結果 | 處置 `2f3df8fc7fc9df4a…`。`OWNER_SEMANTIC_ACK_R26.signed.json` 的 `body.kind=fhs_owner_semantic_accept`、主體 `r26 SHA`；bundle `preauth_at=2026-11-09T19:22:25`（**無 offset**）、`verify_pass_at=20:10:01+08:00`、`signed_at=20:10:02+08:00`。這是具名且排序合理的**作者發佈證物**，但 public key 可讀不等於本 Reviewer 已在原 Host 或已 re-hash 全七簽章。若對時序作密碼學授權判決，前置 timestamp 必須有時區、不可變 WindowID／Nonce 和真正 Key Registry 讀回。\n\n## 5｜FHS-3L&M 任務模型選單：實體資料核對\n\n### 5.1 Owner 模型主備選及交通協定\n\n| Lane / 角色 | `primary`（主選/預設） | `opt1` | `opt2` | 本輪 pool 成員 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **Commander/PLAN** | GPT 6.1 SOL-MEDIUM — `openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol`，OpenAI OAuth，傳輸 bare `gpt-6.1-sol` | DeepSeek V4.1 Flash — `opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash` | **MiMo-V2.6-Flash** — `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash` | 1／2／4，**3/3 靜態入池** |\n| **EXECUTE/Codex bounded writer** | DeepSeek V4.1 Flash — `opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash` | MiMo-V2.6-Flash — `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash` | GPT-6-luna — `openai-codex/gpt-6-luna`，bare `gpt-6-luna` | 1／2／3，**3/3** |\n| **Acceptance Challenge/Independent Officer/VERIFY/SECURITY** | GPT 6.1 SOL-MEDIUM — `openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol` | DeepSeek V4.1 Flash — `opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash` | MiMo-V2.6-Flash — `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash` | 1／3／2，**3/3** |\n\n合計 **9/9「選單可入池」**，已在上傳 `FHS_POOL_CHANGE_R26.json` 及遠端 HGK `config/lane-route-slots.candidate.json` 同時核對。同一模型在不同 Lane 可以合法選取；`same_arm_lanes` 應留紀錄而**不強制異模型、異 Provider，亦不因同一 model 直接拒絕**。此處「已入池」只驗設定（`CANDIDATE` pool），不能無 live request/served/usage 便聲稱有實際服務成功。原 Host 的發送路由由 Owned Codex CLI/Hermes 執行，SBL `--menu` 輸出本身不會轉送任何 API。\n\n### 5.2 r26 實際提供的能力與真正需求差距\n\n真檔 [SBL `tools/fhs_3lm.py`](https://github.com/shw097-team/SBL/blob/1cb8abb0ef118bdd80b08961fd9ca54c657881b9/evidence/fhs-w0-r26-repair-20261109/tools/fhs_3lm.py) 支援 `--menu`、`--bind LANE SLOT`、`--assert BINDING_FILE`、`--assert-arm`、`--allow-fallback`；`--bind` 能生成含 `provider, model, transport_model, registry_sha256, pool_sha256` 的記錄，退役臂有獨立拒絕分支。這是**存在且已有測試的局部 CLI**。它沒有提供 `--spawn` 或強制入口的 Native Host middleware；沒有證據表明其他新 Session／Kanban Child／桌面應用在起動前**必先執行**此 CLI。掛上一段 Prompt/AGENTS 指引不是「硬入口機械攔截」，Host 必須在真正派送之前驗 `SessionBinding`，不可以選擇性忽略。\n\n**額外已重現的綁定缺口：** `BINDING_DIR/f\"{lane}_{slot}.json\"` 同槽多 Session 互相覆寫；`--assert` 比對 registry SHA 及 arm 成員資格，卻**不比對 record 內已綁的 pool SHA、不查 retired_arms、不核 SessionID/WorkID/Nonce/TTL**。`epoch()` 解析 `registry_epoch:` 而真 HGK YAML 使用 `epoch: 41`，輸出的 `epoch` dict 只有 SHA，缺正式數值。腳本還硬編碼 Windows Workspace `C:\\Projects\\Agent_Workspace\\HG-KSEOS`，其所在是 SBL evidence payload，不是已裝載的唯一 Native Router 服務。即使這些瑕疵都修好，仍要真正 Host hook 與 9 份 ActualServed（至少每 Lane 主選與備援受影正反）才可升級為 Cross-Session Hard Contract。\n\n### 5.3 合規的硬入口設計（受界 ChangeSet，而非另建一套 Router）\n\n`Fabric FHS navigation → discover unique FHS-3L&M installed ABI/digest → obtain HGK authoritative registry+pool epoch and SHA → present 3×3 Owner picker → choose explicit/default slot → immutable per Session/Work binding → pre-dispatch validate registry SHA + pool SHA + retired + session/work/ttl/effect → existing Codex CLI/Hermes native dispatcher → independent ActualServed Reader → archive receipt → Verify/Owner (if effect requires)`。\n\n硬約束的是**使用既有入口、單一 authoritative registry、不可繞派權限與證據真值**，不是強迫使用某個模型或 SoD 異模型。若按原 L0 要求 Maker/Issuer/Checker/Publisher 分別使用不同受護 Credential，這是**權限分離**而非模型多樣性；Owner 單人及同模型可合法存在，不能因此把一支通用 Token 變成 Publisher Key。若 Owner 的「不強制 SoD」是打算連受護憑證分離也取消，此方向與原 L0 r4 的 `MakerToken!=Issuer/Checker`、受保護 Checker 不可 Maker 自簽明文不相容，必須獨立治理修訂並逐 Effect 評估，而非本審查官直接宣告藍圖原義已被取代。\n\n## 6｜新增 13 個 Findings（不是重開 W1-W4 全部缺口）\n\n| ID | 分級 | 反證／未達標的具體事實 | 解除所需最小動作 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| **EC-R26-001** | **BLOCKER** | `FHS-3L&M` 在 SBL evidence 隨包存在，但 GitHub/Host 沒有其它 Session **不可繞過**的 native startup/dispatcher hook 真驗收證據 | 原 Host Owner 在唯一 Launcher/dispatcher 中加 machine-enforced discover+bind+assert，fresh PID/codex child 直接繞行 NEG 必擋 |\n| **EC-R26-002** | **HIGH** | `--bind` 固定 `PLAN_opt2.json`，不同 Session 可以覆寫；`--assert` 未鎖 pool SHA、退役名冊、SessionID/TTL，`epoch()` 未取 `epoch:41` 數值 | 原工具**受界修補**唯一 per-run 原子檔、Session/Work/Nonce/TTL、Registry/Pool 雙 SHA、跨進程競態/回放負例；禁止另造 Router |\n| **EC-R26-003** | **HIGH** | G29 原 `evaluate/_valid_votes` 對 `votes_r25` 缺 candidate SHA、假的 Raw SHA、JSON 整數 `1` 票均可能回 `ACCEPTABLE_FOR_FHS_READER_SCOPE`；5 個隔離等價案例 5/5 重現 | `type(x) is bool`、每票 candidate/source/span/raw evidence 必填且真 hash、簽證後不可無效升格；增負例 CI mutation |\n| **EC-R26-004** | **MAJOR** | 真 L1 `6,224,679 bytes`，SBL `FHS_ORIGINAL_ACK.json:subject_scope.source_contract.l1.bytes` 卻為 **111,331**（31 source 的總行數），r25 文稱已修實際未修 | 單次真 `stat/read` 更新 L1 bytes/lines、Source Inventory total_lines 分欄，G24/G29 以錯值負例拒收 |\n| **EC-R26-005** | **BLOCKER FOR MAIN MERGE** | HGK branch `86f40ec...` 與 main `acd919...` 雙向 GitHub Compare HTTP 404 `No common ancestor`；與上傳 `main_is_ancestor=true`、`327 ahead` 不一致 | 完整 `git merge-base`, `rev-list`, parent/root graph, tree/diff，先真實收斂再談 migration；不能快轉假定 |\n| **EC-R26-006** | MAJOR | main registry **epoch 33**、branch **epoch41**、Pool status CANDIDATE，不能以作者的本地「in-force」說成正式 HGK `main` 或實體 Runtime 已生效 | HGK 原 Owner 的 main 衍生受界 PR + Host PolicyEpoch/Actual Reader/rollback |\n| **EC-R26-007** | MAJOR | 9/9 是 pool 成員命中；沒有 PLAN 的 MiMo live inference ActualServed，也沒有九種配置交付成功率資料 | 每個選項先有 Routing/HTTP/Usage/ActualServed 相應正反與 fallbacks 真實 readback |\n| **EC-R26-008** | MAJOR | r26 公開驗章束已具公鑰與 7 簽章，此外部 Chat/Container 尚未自行重算全部 Ed25519；Owner preauth 時間不帶 timezone | 原控制 plane 公鑰 enrollment 與 Canonical Body 的獨立 crypto proof；`preauth_at` 明確 `+08`，防回放 |\n| **EC-R26-009** | MODERATE | `EVIDENCE_SCOPE.json` 的 full profile 不當列六項 excluded，但自報 full Gate 包含六項 | `full.excluded={}`，另獨立寫 `not_runnable_in_CI`；不得重用錯縮寫 |\n| **EC-R26-010** | MODERATE | r26 CH26.5 指 HGK branch 早期 SHA `52c9c21...`，後續整合交付與實測 Head 為 `86f40ec...` | append-only 更正最新 HEAD 及 superseded，而非暗改舊 W0 版 |\n| **EC-R26-011** | MAJOR | r26 ACK `unaffected_proof` 記「32-4=28 properties」，其實四個未受影 WP 是 12+4+2+1=19 clauses=**76 properties** | 用真 Parser/Receipt Count 重算兩種分母，G24 加 typed count check |\n| **EC-R26-012** | GOVERNANCE HOLD | 自由模型選擇與禁令「不可強制 SoD」在**模型差異**層相容；但原 L0 仍明定 protected Checker/Issuer 技術憑證不可 Maker 自證，不能把模型自由偷換成自簽權限 | 原 Owner 明確區分 `model_diversity_optional` 與 `protected_identity_permissions`，對需改者正式分層修訂/安全 NEG |\n| **EC-R26-013** | MAJOR | Fabric r26 已 merge，但候選鏡像未登記 `INTEGRATION/MANIFEST.json`、`effect_permission=NONE`，不能自動變成所有 Session 的強制工程入口 | 原 Fabric Policy Reader admission/manifest、HGK Original Consumer 驗收，不由 SBL MD 宣告 |\n\n完整 JSON 中每個 ID 帶 `subject_sha`、reproduction、命令、exit 語義、實體證據檔與 SHA256。`EC-R26-001/005` 是對 Owner 所問「全面完成／主線合併」的直接阻斷；`EC-R26-003/004` 是新 Gate/證物欠件，須防 false-green。其餘按 `affected_scope` 停止而**不是**禁止其它合法 W0 SOURCE_READ 或 LOCAL_DRAFT_WRITE。\n\n## 7｜驗證器負控與硬入口實際重現\n\n### 7.1 `G29` predicate 重新執行之五例\n\n固定來源：[SBL 原程式 `verify_quorum.py` lines 27–84](https://github.com/shw097-team/SBL/blob/1cb8abb0ef118bdd80b08961fd9ca54c657881b9/evidence/fhs-w0-r26-repair-20261109/tools/verify_quorum.py#L27-L84)。為了在本沙箱沒有 SBL 的 Windows Windows 原件／私鑰且不能修改其源檔，**複製且執行上述判定核心**，清楚標 `PREDICATE_EQUIVALENT_NOT_NATIVE`，不是 import 遠端 Runner 的 full Gate。\n\n```\npython /mnt/data/FHS_W0_R26_ExternalChallenge_Evidence/REPRODUCE_PREDICATE_R26.py\nexit = 0  # 意指反證重現成功，非被審程式合格\ncases = 5 ; reproduced = 5\nraw_sha256 = 492f578c0a6ca890c33e83bcc5178effb5a6e689b5bf0fefeefcea4e0c915c39\n```\n\n| 負控 | 特製輸入 | 原判定核心實際結果 | 判定 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `MISSING_CANDIDATE_SHA_ACCEPTED` | 結構票兩張 true，但每票未給 candidate_sha256 | `ACCEPTABLE_FOR_FHS_READER_SCOPE` | **假綠，待補必填約束** |\n| `TAMPERED_RAW_HASH_NOT_CHECKED` | 投票記錄兩張 true、candidate 正確，但 raw_sha256='00…' 未真重算 | `ACCEPTABLE_FOR_FHS_READER_SCOPE` | **假綠，應驗真 raw** |\n| `INTEGER_ONE_AS_BOOLEAN_ACCEPTED` | JSON value=1，A=1/B=1，非 boolean | `ACCEPTABLE_FOR_FHS_READER_SCOPE` | **型別假綠** |\n| `CORRECTLY_REJECT_STALE` | 一票 stale candidate SHA | `HOLD` | 有效防線已存在 |\n| `CORRECTLY_REJECT_OLD_R24_Q1` | 舊 UNKNOWN/UNKNOWN/true | `HOLD` | r24 主缺陷已修 |\n\n新負控說明**檢查器本身的準入判定會接受不合格格式**，不代表目前已簽的六份真 Receipt 一定含上述缺陷或任何人能繞過 Ed25519。真正負面風險是補丁未把「原 Raw 與候選身份證據」全部以機械真值綁住；因此外審不能宣稱完成 `G29` 全類別外部挑戰。\n\n### 7.2 入口 `--assert` 四例受界重現\n\n固定來源：[SBL 原程式 `fhs_3lm.py` lines 29–35, 73–84, 120–172](https://github.com/shw097-team/SBL/blob/1cb8abb0ef118bdd80b08961fd9ca54c657881b9/evidence/fhs-w0-r26-repair-20261109/tools/fhs_3lm.py#L29-L172)。對獨立隔離的相同判斷式實際重現，沒有對 HGK 真 registry、Session 或主線寫入。\n\n```\npython /mnt/data/FHS_W0_R26_ExternalChallenge_Evidence/REPRODUCE_HARDENTRY_R26.py\nexit = 0 ; cases = 4 ; reproduced = 4\nraw_sha256 = 4542ddf877c06821b1d26614d62505773e801f1dfb4d4bc9a4fe244c72b4fb01\n```\n\n| Case | 必要行為 | 當前程式漏洞 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 調換 Pool SHA、model 未離池 | 必拒 `POOL_DIGEST_DRIFT` | 因 `--assert` 不用 `pool_sha256`，可接受 |\n| 退役 arm 同時仍在 pool | 必拒 `RETIRED_ARM` | `--assert` 不核退役名冊，可接受；`--assert-arm` 是另一獨立子命令，不會自動替它執行 |\n| 兩個 Session 選同 lane/slot | 各有不可覆寫 BindingID | 都會寫 `PLAN_opt2.json`，後 Session 可覆前 Session |\n| `epoch:41` 字段 | 真值進 binding 及 readback | 解析器只查 `registry_epoch:`，數值不入記錄 |\n\n### 7.3 遠端 ACK 數值反證\n\n```\npython /mnt/data/FHS_W0_R26_ExternalChallenge_Evidence/REPRODUCE_REMOTE_METADATA_CONFLICT.py\nexit = 0  # 成功重現資料不一致\nactual L1 bytes = 6,224,679\nSBL published ACK .subject_scope.source_contract.l1.bytes = 111,331\nraw_sha256=5bf44eb0f4b6433685775eacb0ffd85dcf466390da53f281a6aca1a8a6b63118\n```\n\n該測試以 connector readback 的固定欄位為受檢聲明、本機原始 L1 bytes 為真值；未測遠端 JSON 的全部欄位或全項修補。**修補後應在原 G24 增加對這個欄位的實體 Raw bytes 校驗**，否則 `G24 PASS` 不能證明證物中所有 `bytes` 均實測一致。\n\n## 8｜HGK `main` epoch 33、in-force 41、「327 commits」：建議裁決\n\n### 8.1 不建議一次性大量合併，也不建議現在切 default branch\n\n**本輪確實固定讀到：** HGK `main=acd9196f...` 的 `config/lane-routing-registry.yaml` 是 `status:CANDIDATE, epoch:33`，HGK 分支 `config/plan-mimo-flash-20261109@86f40ec...` 中同路徑是 `status:IN_FORCE, epoch:41`；`lane-route-slots.candidate.json` `status:CANDIDATE`，`model-lane-assignment.json` revision43，退休臂是 `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-pro`。作者聲明「main 祖先、領先 327 commits」屬作者 sidecar；本輪 GitHub 官方 Compare API 用 exact 40 字元 SHA 雙向呼叫，均返回 `404 No common ancestor`。因此，`327` **不能作為已外部證明的 main fast-forward range 或一次性 merge 尺寸**。事實也可能涉及長期分歧、不同根、replace refs 或本機只比對自身 lineage；須查雙方完整 DAG 才能分類。\n\n### 8.2 選定方案：`分批受界回填（Main-derived bridge PR）`\n\n| 方案 | 建議 | 理由 |\n|---|---|---|\n| **A. 一次大量合併 327 Commit** | **REJECT / NOT_AUTHORIZED** | 未確認共同祖先；Issuer、ACL、PolicyEpoch、license、依賴、CI/World effect 全混到一次不可清楚回退的操作；有 unrelated histories 風險 |\n| **B. 按相依和風險分批，從現行 main 建受界橋接 PR** | **RECOMMEND** | 有效保留 GitHub `main` 公開歷史與 PR 追溯，先救必要 registry/retired/3LM，再封存其餘行為差額，逐批真測與回退 |\n| **C. 直接改用 in-force 血緣為 GitHub `main`** | **HOLD** | GitHub 原始遠端根尚不一致；直接改 default branch／force-ref 容易破 Fabric/SBL source pins、原消費者及未計重的安全／商業行為；除非更高治理遷移另行批准 |\n\n### 8.3 H0–H5 一次性完整操作計畫\n\n| Stage | 動作與原負責者 | 必需完成證據與負例 |\n|---|---|---|\n| **H0 固定兩個 SHA / 找共同祖先** | HGK Owner 對 clone 同時 `git cat-file -p`, `git merge-base`, `git log --all --graph`, `git rev-list --left-right --count`，核對 GitHub 404；查 shallow/graft/replace/remote/ref。不得臆稱 327。 | 兩端完全 DAG、git object hash、對比結論（SAME_ROOT/DISJOINT/UNKNOWN）與 Raw，外部讀回相同 SHA |\n| **H1 對所有差額做受影分類** | `config/registry`, `lane pool`, assignment, launcher/dispatcher, tests, Issuer, Policy, Native World, license/deps, consumer pins 對照；327 若屬真實差額，**每 Commit 有處置**。 | `affected_paths, original_owner, effect, dependency, test, revert, disposition` 台帳；未分類 HOLD |\n| **H2 第一批 P0 Route+Retire** | 從 GitHub `main` 開新 branch，經 HGK 原 Domain Writer 搬移 Owner 授權之 epoch41／九槽／禁用 pro；不直接 cherry-pick 全 branch，不偷偷造第二 Router。 | CI/原 Host policy validate + diff-live、retired 反例、所有九槽 metadata/actual pin；能 rollback；若 H0 不同根，仍可採內容差額移植 |\n| **H3 第二批 Host/密鑰/依賴** | 有界補 cryptography/PyYAML 依賴、root License/NOTICE disposition、原 Registry/NATIVE ACL/受護 writer 導線；按 Effective Source Authority 逐項合併。 | clean install、缺包 fail closed、Authority/Issuer negative、各 original consumer ACK；不把 G16b 開口說已閉 |\n| **H4 Fabric/SBL 原域接線** | 待 HGK 確定主線，Fabric Policy Reader 處理 r26 mirror admission，SBL 真 Runner/Verifier 讀雙 SHA、PR CI。 | 原 `FHS-NAV` 仍唯讀；原 Domain Reader 決定 effective，不能一個跨庫隱形 commit |\n| **H5 原 Native cutover** | Owner preauth→PLAN/EXECUTE bounded writer→VERIFY independent→有權 Owner/Issuer receipt→受界 PR merge→Host Current Pointer readback→Rollback plan。 | GitHub `main`、Host installed registry、ActualServed/PolicyEpoch 三者一致；原相應 World effect 才准給 Native 相關 PASS |\n\n**禁止** `git merge --allow-unrelated-histories` 未經鑑識直接全合、直接 force push main、用 `source status:IN_FORCE` 推斷 host loaded；不可把未批准的 327 個提交當 W0 一次 pool 調整的副作用。H0 沒真結論前，B 方案的 H2 僅準備新受界 PR、不升級有效控制面。\n\n## 9｜W0 完整結案與 L0/L1/L2/W1–W4 回寫裁決\n\n### 9.1 寫進回合目錄的檔案再還原（`gate_r19.py:1520-1549`）。\n  在 `--sandbox read-only` 下第一寫入即被拒，於是「突變會 FIRED」與「已還原」只有具寫入權的作者能主張。\n- 修法：`gate_r20.py` 的 `selftest()` 先 `mirror_round()` 複製到 `%TEMP%`，所有 baseline 與 mutated\n  執行都讀副本；結束時比對 `tree_fingerprint()` 前後值，相等才輸出 `round_tree_unchanged: true`。\n- 證據：`out/GATE_R20_SELFTEST.json` 的 `wrote_inside_round`、`round_tree_unchanged`、\n  `round_tree_fingerprint_before/after`。\n- 自傷紀錄：第一次修法用 `FHS_G27_TEXT` 環境變數傳 621 KB 文件，撞上 Windows 32,767 字元上限，\n  自測回報 `G27 ERROR:ValueError`。改成模組層級 `_G27_TEXT_OVERRIDE` 後才 FIRED。\n\n### CE-08 `r19-label-scope`\n\n- 病因：`label_discipline.py` v1 只掃 markdown 與 JSON 的 `label|kind|claim` 三個鍵（大小寫敏感），\n  於是 `body.kind = \"original_reader_ack\"` 與 `consequence` 散文欄位完全在視野之外。\n- 修法：v2 走訪 `out/*.json`、`receipts/**/*.json`、`evidence/*.json` 的每一個字串值；合格前綴為\n  `FHS_`、`BARE_`，或是一條明示箭頭的改名紀錄（`裸 token -> fhs_ token`）；`not_claimed` /\n  `*_verbatim` 鍵下的字串屬否定容器或逐字引述，不算宣稱。掃描面寫在 `out/SCAN_SCOPE.json`，\n  每個排除都附理由；試圖排除 `receipts/`、`out/PRINCIPAL_DEFINITION.json`、\n  `out/FHS_ORIGINAL_ACK.json` 的 scope 會被拒絕（`SCOPE_EXCLUDES_A_MUST_SCAN_ARTEFACT`）。\n- 證據：`out/LABEL_DISCIPLINE_R20.json`（`machine_count: 0`）、`tools/gate_r20.py` 的 `G28`。\n\n## 3. 閘 v6（21 項）\n\n`G10,G11,G12,G13,G14,G15,G16a,G16b,G16c,G17,G18,G19,G20,G21,G22,G23,G24,G25,G26,G27,G28`\n\n新增 `G28 MACHINE_READABLE_QUALIFIER`（BLOCKING）。`G16b` RCA 才能使用、每次重編 L0–L3、所有普通 Goal 都上 Kanban/Swarm、建立第二 HGK 或 PIPD、Maker 自行生成允許憑證、以 50 H1 當日常 50 Gate、以 `/ship` 代 Publisher、把遙端原始碼等於本機 Loaded、因本輪寫一份 L0 就宣稱產品 Released。\n114: \n115: ### 01.4｜Product SPEC 最小完整交付（可供 L1 Product Reader 接收）115: ### 01.4｜Product SPEC 最小完整交付（可供 L1 Product Reader 接收）\n116: \n117: - **Persona**：唯一 Human Owner，單機 Windows 工作站；使用普通對話／Hermes Session／Codex 和同一套原三庫，既可進行 A-plane 自身建置，也能做 B-plane 日常任務。\n118: - **JTBD**：以最少重複決策及工具切換，從 Goal 得到真 Consumer 可用結果並在可預期失敗時有清楚停點與恢復；高自動化不等於跳過必要權限。\n119: - **首個可量測 FUO（A）**：`WP-FHS-15` 三份 Product/Architecture/Program SPEC 在獨立 DraftRoot 產出、包含 SourceSHA/OriginalConsumer/POS/NEG/NonGoal，位元組 readback；若原 PIPD Reader 未 ACK，標 `DRAFT_FUO` 而非 `PI_ACCEPTED`。當 `WP00→WP01→WP05` 原前置閉合且原 Compiler 真讀入，才聲稱 `ORIGINAL_READER_ACCEPTED`。120: - **首個可量測 FUO（B）**：依 TaskKind/Effect 選 `source-bound 答案`、`SpecDelta`、`可逆 Repo patch`、`有 WorkID 的續跑成果` 或 `獲准的 Native World Effect`；單純讀取不需 OPS Permit。\n121: - **Scope**：八 Kind、三 Mode、四 Host 保留原 Raw Token/ProviderOff；`Kind×Mode×Host` 不全域默認 96/192 格都能執行。保留 16 Moments、38 Delivery Surfaces、39 ModeGate、原三 SP/六模組的 OriginalID。\n122: - **Non-goals**：不打造第二 Agent OS/Compiler/Issuer/Queue/Checker/Publisher；不為每個 Goal 編譯 31 冊或執行所有 282 Case；不以文件編制或 GitHub 綠燈宣稱 Host 已資格化。\n123: - **ACC-P**：同一 SourceSHA/Host 的原 Consumer 收到可解析、可撤回、非空的 FUO；**NEG-P**：拿 485 身份當 485 install、使 `A-Plane` 壓迫 B-plane READ 或把 Parent `不得` 改成 `可以` 均判 `PRODUCT_SEMANTIC_DOWNGRADE`。\n124: 131: | 文檔流程 | L0→L1 完整來源設計→L2 動態施工波次→L3 當波包 | READ／DRAFT 可短路；真正工程時按受影走 TPM/PIPD／原 Gate |\n132: | FUO | W0-A WP15 三 SPEC＋原 PIPD 有效接收候選或明確 HOLD；W0-B 再做受保護 GJ02 | 原 Consumer 真可用結果，不必先建完整產品 |\n133: | 驗收 | 全產品 identity 保全、必要者分波 Native、原 Checker/Publisher 局部放行 | 所涉效果及適用原 Oracle 的最低充分驗收，不執行無關測例 |\n134: | 失敗 | WorkID，崩潰恢復先對 Operation/World reconcile。\n142: 5. `PROTECTED_HOST_OR_EXTERNAL`：OPS／外部資料效果需原 Host ABI/OS Token/Issuer/Consent/回復，不以 Repo revert 當 Host rollback。\n143: 6. Qualification。\n1185: \n1186: ## 08｜TPM／PIPD 製程、Canonical Writer 與 L0 對下游的合法接口1200: **[PLUGIN/SOURCE]** `$sbl-tpm-sp` `tpm-product-plan`、`tpm-system-plan`、`tpm-program-plan` 的 `GHP-01 once` 僅作**同 Subject/SourceManifestSHA/Scope 的一次性原 Decision Reader 查核**，已存在且有效便復用，沒有新決策不可每張 Kanban 卡重問。只要合法 SOURCE_READ／LOCAL_DRAFT_WRITE，`GHP-01=UNDECIDED/DEFERRED/NOT_OBSERVED` 可作有界三 SPEC 草稿、繼續作者端審查；但任何需要 Owner 真 ratification 的受影 formal promotion 仍保持 `OWNER_DECISION_HOLD`。原 GHP-01 的有效決議與 r2 的 `Q01–Q10 PROPOSAL` 是兩種不同身份，後者不自動變 `DECIDED`。\n1201: \n1202: **原接收例（Sidecar 不污染 strict payload）**：`subject+source_manifest_sha+scope+GHP01_readback_ref+QuestionLedger_snapshot` → PM/SE/TPgM Product/Architecture/Program SPEC Draft → `Original PIPD Intake/Authority/Profile Reader`。若來源/Scope 改變，只重判受影 GHP01 與 KnownAnswer，輸出 `changed_decision_ids`；`CR-DOC04-STATE-MAP` 未解時 T4/T5 formal promotion `HOLD`，T3 Draft 不需假收據。正式遠端 Publish/Release **才**由原 Publisher/GHP-02 取得真 Admission 與 World readback；不設自家 Approval 系統。\n1203: \n1204: **POS**：同一 Qualified Owner Decision 一次使用、同鍵未重問，Reader 收到不同三 SPEC；**NEG**：`GHP-01` 每子任務重跑、`DEFERRED` 自升 Accepted、`@2` SourceTrace Sidecar 塞入 canonical PI top-level、`GHP-02` 缺席仍 Release→分別 `REDUNDANT_OWNER_PROMPT / STAGE_GUARD_VIOLATION / PI_SCHEMA_NACK / PUBLISHER_HOLD`。`tpm-handoff` 僅交原 Receiver 的 versioned Candidate，沒有新 WorkID/Permit/Checker。1227: - 需要受治理設計：三 SPEC＋原 PIPD 必要閉包；PI 可以先經原 Reader，PD 只在真的 RepoContext 有效時晚綁；本輪不必為其他 948 原子編 PI。\n1228: - 需要有實效：對受影 OperationType 取原 Registry、Effect ECP、Issuer、World、相應 Checker；沒有原 Issuer 就只停效果，不停已完成 Spec。\n1229: - 需要發行：同 Candidate 的原 Protected Check 和 Publisher 独立 Gate；不能用同一 Owner 的文字 YES 代替不同技術身份。\n1230: \n1231: ### 08.4｜已讀的 `$sbl-tpm-sp` 契約與正式 PIPD Reader 的關鍵差異1233: 本次實際讀到的 `tpm-sp`, `tpm-setup`, `tpm-product-plan`, `tpm-system-plan`, `tpm-program-plan`, `pipd-route-intake`, `pipd-authority-source`, `pipd-pi-compile`, `pipd-pd-bind`, `pipd-execution-contract`, `pipd-assurance-tqaep`, `pipd-package-project`, `tpm-handoff` 均是 **[PLUGIN SKILL CONTRACT]**，不是本機啟用的原 Compiler 或其正式 Writer。其 `PRE-V2 active route (@2)` 明確要求原 Strict Schema 不被 sidecar 混入、`KnownAnswer` 同 Subject/SourceSHA/Scope 復用、使用原 PIPD Writer/Reader 綁定 Canonical PI/PD/ECP/TQAEP，欠原 Reader 則 `EXECUTOR_UNBOUND`。在本 L0 合法的作用只有規劃三 SPEC、產品/架構/計畫封閉性及驗收的預期證據。\n1234: \n1235: 原正式製程（下游才可執行）：`Goal + SourceLocator → typed IntentCard + AuthorityBinding / SourceFreeze → ProfileBinding (safety veto) → Original PI-PKG → live RepoContext late bind Original PD-PKG → DEV/OPS/USER/RELEASE 正交 ECP → Original TQAEP runner/oracle → Fabric/HGK formal effect → Protected Checker → Original Publisher`。每一個 arrow 需明確 `producer, consumer, schema $id, SameSubject, source_digest, permission_basis, output_sha, ACK/NACK, first_fail`；任何原 Reader 或真 Repo/Host 欠件時，只輸出 `HOLD_AFFECTED_SCOPE_ONLY`，不組造代替授權的 JSON。\n1236: \n1237: 1301: ### 09.7A｜四表與 `partition_manifest`、三 SpecDelta、八只讀 View 的唯一權責（F06）\n1302: \n1303: r4 的 **四張 JSONL 是唯一候選事實表**，不是唯一必要抽取物：`source_registry.jsonl / obligation_registry.jsonl / semantic_relations.jsonl / decision_delta_log.jsonl` 為不重複人工維護的實質事實資料；`partition_manifest` 是 U0 byte-closure 輔助證據；`run_manifest/cursor/checkpoint` 是可恢復執行控制；`ProductSpecDelta/ArchitectureSpecDelta/ProgramSpecDelta` 是 U3 作者側規格交接；**八個只讀 View 從相同 SourceSet/RunID 的四表+原權限 Reader 派生**，並非可獨立簽核的 Canonical 資料庫。\n1304: \n1305: | 原 r4 Read-only View | 衍生欄／受影分母 | 原 Reader／Consumer | 專屬 NEG |1333: ### 09.9 七個 Coverage 與唯一 ReleaseAuthorization\n1334: Repo／Source／PIPD／HGK／Hermes／Checker 的實際 Reader；本 L0 定義**聯接查詢視圖**而非建立第二 SSOT：`Source Ledger（原 Source）; Requirement Ledger（原 OriginalID/Clause）; Owner Decision Ledger（原 Question/Scope）; PIPD Ledger（原 Writer）; Work/Operation Ledger（原 HGK）; Host/Model Ledger（原 PID/ActualServed）; Case/Oracle Ledger（原 Original Registry）; Evidence Ledger（真 Raw）; Delivery Ledger（原 Consumer/Publisher）`。每一 Event/Raw 保留 `producer,readback,content_digest,subject,candidate_sha,scope,host,policy_epoch,effect,original_owner,first_fail`；不把數個來源剪貼成可寫入的新「FHS 全域 Canonical DB」。\n1407: \n1408: ### 10.5 DELIVER：可收、可用、可恢復的接受準則1411: - **工程候選**：原 Repo/Host 可讀、exact WriteSet、TaskSpecSeed/PI/PD/ECP/TQAEP 預期資料都可交原 Writer/Consumer，若 NACK 則附 first fail 和恢復邊，不以文件格式合格自封 Native ready。\n1412: - **真產品功能**：GJ01/GJ02/GJ03 或符合 Kind/Mode/Host 的真原 Consumer 能操作並回 Raw，且具安全退出。沒有該原 Consumer 就是 `FUNCTION_NOT_VERIFIED`，不是文件已寫即可 Pass。\n1413: - **Independent/Release**：須不同 Principal Original Checker 真同主體驗收，原 Publisher 另有發行權與可回復 Readback；兩者永不透過 GPT/CI/捐贈工具代簽。\n1414: \n1415: 1420: | Tier/Effect | 何時發生 | 最小必需 POS／NEG/Raw | 不必全加的門檻 |\n1421: |---|---|---|---|\n1422: | L1/SOURCE_READ | 純答案、索引、遠端唯讀查證 | 原 SourceSHA/ACL、無寫入，偽 SHA/過期 Reader NEG | 不需 HGK OPS Permit/Independent Publisher |\n1423: | L0/LOCAL_DRAFT_WRITE | 隔離 SPEC/問答/方案候選 | DraftRoot ACL、newfile/hash/readback/clean residue；逃逸目錄 NEG | 不需真 Repo PD 或 B1 全 Case |\n1424: | DEV/Repo effect | 受界 Patch、有效 code 變更 | Live RepoHEAD+dirty/untracked/preimage/writeSet、Original DEV ECP、Diff、測例/rollback；path escape NEG | 不需無關 Host install/全產品 949 tests |1425: | OPS/HOST/外部 effect | OS、Policy、Native Worker/網路/帳戶效果 | 原受保護 Token/ACL/Permit、PolicyEpoch、OperationID、WorldRaw/compensation；過期權限 NEG | 只跑受影 Host/Case/安全根 |\n1426: | USER／資料影響 | Subject/Candidate/Scope、正式 Publisher/Release Readback | 不把 CI green/gstack `/ship` 當原 Publisher |\n1428: \n1429: **TQAEP**：每個受影 OriginalID 的 POS/NEG/FirstFail、Runner/Oracle/Raw Schema/Host/ActualServed 由原 Registry 讀入；`RUNNER_UNBOUND` 時僅可設計，不簽 `INDEPENDENT_PASS`。高風險變更沿原 Trust 邊界回 Root Checker；不擴成所有常態任務全套運行。1430: \n1431: \n1432: ## 11｜單人 Owner 的分工、原權限、決策與責任邊界\n1433: \n1434: ### 11.1 一位 Human Owner 不等於一組共用高權限憑證1434: ### 11.1 一位 Human Owner 不等於一組共用高權限憑證\n1435: \n1436: **[SOURCE/PROPOSAL]** FHS 以「唯一單人單機 Human Owner」為產品與操作假設；人數只有一位，不表示技術 Principal 可以合併。使用者可核定目標、受影風險、安裝／維護時段與正式發行意圖；真正的 Maker、HGK Issuer、Original Policy Owner、獨立 Protected Checker、Original Publisher，仍各自使用由既有 Host/OS/原 Registry 管制的技術憑證與生效流程。無法證明技術分離的受保護操作保持 `H-OS-08`，不得讓 GPT 代理用角色名稱自簽。\n1437: \n1438: | 身分或角色 | 可負責 | 不可代替 | 需要核對的原 Reader／責任 |1438: | 身分或角色 | 可負責 | 不可代替 | 需要核對的原 Reader／責任 |\n1439: |---|---|---|---|\n1440: | Human Owner（單一） | Goal、可見風險、原 User Consent、最小許可範圍、各 Question 決策採否 | 原 Issuer、受保護獨立 Checker 的技術憑證及正式 Publisher 無條件認證 | Human Owner decision／Consent Reader |\n1441: | GPT-6 動態分析角色 `FHS-R00..31` | 來源保全、產品／系統／程序設計、版本衝突揭露、受界建議稿 | Native Principal、OS 身分、已生效 Human Owner × 不同技術 Principal 的允許／拒絕／待核矩陣（F07／F16）\n1451: \n1452: **[SOURCE/PROPOSAL]** `HumanOwnerCount=1` 與 `HGK_LogicalIssuerCount=1` 是產品配置方向，不表示 `MakerToken=IssuerToken=CheckerToken=PublisherToken`。原 Windows SID／ACL、密鑰可讀/可寫、原 Receiver Registry/PolicyEpoch/原 Checker/Pub Reader 是判定條件；本 L0 沒有實機 OS 或真 Token Reader，不能聲稱已證分離。即使同一 Human 輪流操作不同合法 Service，正式 SoD 仍由原不同受信技術 Principal/受保護憑證/規章決定；若原領域硬性要求兩位真人，則只對該 Protected Scope `INDEPENDENCE_HOLD`，不得自創第二位 Owner 或採模型人格作替代。\n1453: \n1454: | 原 Operation/Effect | 實際適用技術身分 | 要回讀的 OS/Reader/證據 | 本 L0 **必要主機讀回欄**：`OperationType, OS_SID, runtime_token_acl, issuer_principal, issuer_key_writable_by_maker?, checker_key_readable_by_maker?, checker_credential_reader_ref, original_publisher_reader_ref, ttl, consent_scope, denied_mutation_raw, subject, candidate_sha, host, policy_epoch`。`ALLOW` 僅是合法低效果/已有有效權限的**設計分流**，不是授予新權限。若同一帳戶能任意修改受保護 Credential Store，需由原 Trust/OS Owner 修復 ACL 分離，再由 Protected Checker 獨立驗；本 L0 只允標 `OS_SOD_FAIL`，不以換 Profile 名稱解除。1558: ### 12.3 首個合法切片：用戶先得到真可用成果\n1559: \n1560: **普通 Goal（非發行）**例如「讀已內建的 SBL14/PIPD 來源與 KnownAnswer，產生一份原 PIPD 能審閱的產品變更規格」：第一步在原 ACL 下讀源；第二步由 TPM 三 SPEC 草稿產出來源綁定 SpecDelta；第三步**只有 `WP00→WP01→WP05` 的原 Authority/Oracle/Profile 前置被合法讀回且原 Canonical PIPD Reader 真 ACK**，才得到 `WP05_ORIGINAL_READER_ACCEPTED`；否則停在 `EXECUTOR_UNBOUND` 的可讀草稿 `WP15_DRAFT_FUO`，二者 Claim 分開。只有要寫入真正 Repo 的部分才送原 PD live bind，當涉及 OPS/USER/RELEASE，再送各自原 ECP、HGK Admission/Consent/Publisher。這是 **B-Plane 第一 FUO**，不是要求先做完 50 章、949 Atom 與全部 282 Runner 的 Native PASS。\n1561: \n1562: ### 12.4 Draft→定案→返工：受界閉環1562: ### 12.4 Draft→定案→返工：受界閉環\n1563: \n1564: 在 L3 的已准受影範圍，資料流為：`原 RequirementID` → `source-bound draft` → `spec/writeSet review` → `original Reader A line in `AGENTS.md` is a prompt,\nnot an interception. This module is the interception: a host that spawns a lane must pass\n`assert-entry` (binding re-verified from bytes, arm checked against the retirement ledger, the SBL\nbinding reader consulted BY PATH) and then hold a one-shot run-scoped capability that the dispatcher\nCONSUMES before it creates any child.\n\nDivision of labour (deliberate; do not grow this file into a router)\n--------------------------------------------------------------------\n* The **routing decision** is not made here. This module makes no selection, writes no registry and\n  keeps no second copy of the pool. It reads `config/lane-routing-registry.yaml` and\n  `config/lane-route-slots.candidate.json` for their BYTES (sha256, numeric epoch) and for the\n  retirement ledger, and it reads one lane's ordered pool only to name the rank-1 arm when the caller\n  did not name an arm.\n* The **binding predicate** (\"is this binding still admissible?\") is NOT re-implemented here. It is\n  executed by the single-source reader — `fhs_3lm.py --assert <binding>` — invoked by PATH\n  (`--binding-reader`, else `$FHS_3LM_PATH`, else `config/fhs-3lm-path.txt`, else\n  `var/fhs-entry/binding-reader-path.txt`). Its refusal codes are MAPPED into this gate's namespace\n  (`READER_CODE_MAP`), never guessed.\n* This module owns exactly four things the reader cannot be trusted to be complete about on every\n  revision: the registry/pool DIGEST the binding was minted against (drift), the retirement ledger\n  lookup for the arm about to be spawned, the arm the caller is actually asking for (the binding may\n  name a different one), and expiry. Everything else is the reader's verdict, quoted.\n\nFail-closed codes (each with its own exit status; nothing falls through to a spawn)\n----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    20 FHS_ENTRY_ASSERT_REQUIRED    no --binding supplied, or the file is absent/unreadable\n    21 FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID    not a binding record / tampered / reader refused it / wrong shape\n    22 FHS_ENTRY_EPOCH_DRIFT        registry bytes (or its numeric epoch) moved since the binding\n    23 FHS_ENTRY_POOL_DRIFT         pool bytes moved since the binding\n    24 FHS_ENTRY_RETIRED_ARM        the requested arm is in the retirement ledger\n    25 FHS_ENTRY_ARM_MISMATCH       the requested arm is not the bound arm (or the token's arm)\n    26 FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_EXPIRED    the binding's own expires_at has passed\n    27 FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED   the binding reader / HGK control plane could not be resolved\n    28 FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_REQUIRED     no one-shot capability for this run_id (absent or expired)\n    29 FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_CONSUMED     the capability was already spent (replay / re-mint)\n\nClaim ceiling\n-------------\nA pass here proves that THIS host's declared dispatch path verified a binding and spent a run-scoped\ncapability before creating a child. It does not bind a desktop-app session and cannot be enforced on\nanother host that does not adopt this ABI; nothing here makes a model call or contacts a relay.\n\nExit codes: 0 ok; 2 bad usage (argparse); 20-29 as above.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport datetime\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport pathlib\nimport sys\n\nSCHEMA = \"FHS-ENTRY-GUARD/1\"\nCAPABILITY_SCHEMA = \"FHS-ENTRY-CAPABILITY/1\"\nBINDING_SCHEMA_HINT = \"FHS.3LM.Binding\"\nROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nDEFAULT_ENTRY_DIR = ROOT / \"var\" / \"fhs-entry\"\nDEFAULT_TTL_SECONDS = 300\nREADER_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 180\n\nEXIT_CODES = {\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_ASSERT_REQUIRED\": 20,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\": 21,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_EPOCH_DRIFT\": 22,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_POOL_DRIFT\": 23,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_RETIRED_ARM\": 24,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_ARM_MISMATCH\": 25,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_EXPIRED\": 26,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED\": 27,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_REQUIRED\": 28,\n    \"FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_CONSUMED\": 29,\n}\n\n# The reader's own vocabulary -> this gate's namespace. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef sha256_bytes(blob: bytes) -> str:\n    return hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef sha256_file(path) -> str | None:\n    try:\n        with open(path, \"rb\") as fh:\n            h = hashlib.sha256()\n            for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b\"\"):\n read_json(path) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:\n    try:\n        raw = pathlib.Path(path).read_bytes()\n    except OSError as exc:\n        return None, f\"unreadable: {type(exc).__name__}: isinstance(doc, dict):\n        return None, f\"top-level value is {type(doc).__name__}, not an object\"\n    return doc, None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# control-plane bytes: registry (YAML, scanned) + pool (JSON)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef scan_registry(path) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Read the registry's BYTES and the few fields the gate owns.\n\n    YAML is not parsed as a whole (PyYAML is not a stdlib dependency and a full parse would be a\n    second, competing reader). Only top-level scalars and the two-space keys of the top-level\n    `retired_arms:` block are picked up, which is exactly what this gate may decide on: the epoch,\n    the status, and the retirement ledger.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = {\"path\": str(path), \"sha256\": sha256_file(path), \"epoch\": None, \"epoch_raw\": None,\n           \"status\": None, \"contract_epoch\": None, \"retired\": {}, \"error\": None}\n    try:\n        text = pathlib.Path(path).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n    except OSError as exc:\n        out[\"error\"] = f\"unreadable: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\"\n        return out\n    in_retired = False\n    for line in text.splitlines():\n        if not line.strip() or line.lstrip().startswith(\"#\"):\n            continue\n        if not line[0].isspace():\n            key, _, value = line.partition(\":\")\n            key = key.strip()\n            in_retired = key == \"retired_arms\"\n            if key in (\"epoch\", \"status\", \"contract_epoch\"):\n                out[key if key != \"epoch\" else \"epoch_raw\"] = value.strip().strip(\"'\\\"\")\n            continue\n        if in_retired and line.startswith(\"  \") and not line.startswith(\"   \") and \":\" in line:\n            arm = line.strip().split(\":\", 1)[0].strip().strip(\"'\\\"\")\n            if arm:\n                out[\"retired\"][arm] = out[\"retired\"].get(arm) or {}\n    raw = out.pop(\"epoch_raw\", None)\n    if isinstance(raw, str) and raw.lstrip(\"-\").isdigit():\n        out[\"epoch\"] = int(raw)\n    elif raw is not None:\n        out[\"error\"] = out[\"error\"] or f\"epoch is not numeric: {raw!r}\"\n        out[\"epoch\"] = raw\n    return out\n\n\ndef scan_pool(path) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Read the pool's BYTES, its numeric epoch, its lanes (rank order) and its retirement ledger.\"\"\"\n    out = {\"path\": str(path), \"sha256\": sha256_file(path), \"epoch\": None, \"lanes\": {},\n           \"retired\": {}, \"error\": None}\n    doc, err = read_json(path)\n    if doc is None:\n        out[\"error\"] = err\n        return out\n    epoch = doc.get(\"epoch\")\n    out[\"epoch\"] = epoch if isinstance(epoch, int) and not isinstance(epoch, bool) else None\n    if out[\"epoch\"] is None and epoch is not None:\n        out[\"error\"] = f\"pool epoch is not an integer: {epoch!r}\"\n    retired = doc.get(\"retired_arms\")\n    if isinstance(retired, dict):\n        out[\"retired\"] = {str(k): (v if isinstance(v, dict) else {}) for k, v in retired.items()}\n    for lane in doc.get(\"lanes\") or []:\n        if not isinstance(lane, dict):\n            continue\n        name = lane.get(\"lane\")\n        if not isinstance(name, str):\n            continue\n        rows = []\n        for entry in lane.get(\"ordered_pool\") or []:\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                continue\n            arm = f\"{entry.get('provider')}/{entry.get('model')}\"\n            rows.append({\"arm\": arm, \"rank\": entry.get(\"rank\"),\n                         \"dispatchable\": entry.get(\"dispatchable\") is not False\n                         and not str(entry.get(\"feasibility\") or \"\").startswith(\n                             (\"UNREACHABLE\", \"CONFIG_COMPLETE_NOT_ACTIVATED\"))})\n        out[\"lanes\"][name] = rows\n    return out\n\n\ndef resolve_hgk_root(explicit=None) -> tuple[pathlib.Path | None, str]:\n    \"\"\"--hgk-root, then $HGK_ROOT, then config/hgk-root.txt, then this repo (which carries both files).\"\"\"\n    candidates: list[tuple[pathlib.Path, str]] = []\n    if explicit:\n        candidates.append((pathlib.Path(explicit), \"flag:--hgk-root\"))\n    env = os.environ.get(\"HGK_ROOT\")\n    if env:\n        candidates.append((pathlib.Path(env), \"env:HGK_ROOT\"))\n    discovery = ROOT / \"config\" / \"hgk-root.txt\"\n    if discovery.is_file():\n        for line in discovery.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines():\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line and not line.startswith(\"#\"):\n                candidates.append((pathlib.Path(line), f\"discovery:{discovery.name}\"))\n                break\n    candidates.append((ROOT, \"repo-root\"))\n    for path, source in candidates:\n        if (path / \"config\" / \"lane-routing-registry.yaml\").is_file():\n            return path.resolve(), source\n    return None, \"unresolved\"\n\n\ndef resolve_reader(explicit=None) -> tuple[pathlib.Path | None, str]:\n    \"\"\"--binding-reader, then $FHS_3LM_PATH, then the two discovery files, else unresolved.\"\"\"\n    candidates: list[tuple[pathlib.Path, str]] = []\n    if explicit:\n        candidates.append((pathlib.Path(explicit), \"flag:--binding-reader\"))\n    env = os.environ.get(\"FHS_3LM_PATH\")\n    if env:\n        candidates.append((pathlib.Path(env), \"env:FHS_3LM_PATH\"))\n    for rel in (\"config/fhs-3lm-path.txt\", \"var/fhs-entry/binding-reader-path.txt\"):\n        path = ROOT / rel\n        if not path.is_file():\n            continue\n        for line in path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines():\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line and not line.startswith(\"#\"):\n                candidates.append((pathlib.Path(line), f\"discovery:{path.name}\"))\n                break\n    for path, source in candidates:\n        if path.is_file():\n            return path.resolve(), source\n    return None, \"unresolved\"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# the binding reader (single-source predicate; called by path only)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef call_reader(reader: pathlib.Path, binding: pathlib.Path, hgk_root: pathlib.Path) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Run `<reader> --assert <binding>` and return its raw verdict.\n\n    The reader resolves its own control plane from `--hgk-root` then `$HGK_ROOT`; this gate hands the\n    RESOLVED root through `$HGK_ROOT` so the two can never disagree about which bytes are authority\n    (and so a reader revision that has no `--hgk-root` flag is not fed an argument it would reject).\n    \"\"\"\n    import subprocess\n\n    env = dict(os.environ)\n    env[\"HGK_ROOT\"] = str(hgk_root)\n    argv = [sys.executable, str(reader), \"--assert\", str(binding)]\n    rec = {\"path\": str(reader), \"argv\": argv[1:], \"hgk_root_env\": str(hgk_root)}\n    try:\n        proc = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding=\"utf-8\",\n                              errors=\"replace\", env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,\n                              timeout=READER_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)\n    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:\n        rec.update(exit=None, ok=False, code=\"FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED\",\n                   detail=f\"the binding reader could not be executed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n        return rec\n    rec[\"exit\"] = proc.returncode\n    rec[\"stdout_sha256\"] = sha256_bytes((proc.stdout or \"\").encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    body = None\n    try:\n        body = json.loads(proc.stdout or \"\")\n    except ValueError:\n        body = None\n    rec[\"stdout\"] = (proc.stdout or \"\")[:4000]\n    if proc.returncode == 0:\n        rec.update(ok=True, code=None, detail=None, body=body)\n        return rec\n    reader_code = None\n    if isinstance(body, dict):\n        reader_code = body.get(\"code\") or body.get(\"error\") or body.get(\"first_fail\")\n    guard_code = READER_CODE_MAP.get(str(reader_code)) or READER_EXIT_MAP.get(proc.returncode)\n    if not guard_code:\n        # The reader did not REFUSE the binding -- it failed to produce a verdict at all (a traceback, a\n        # usage error, an unusable revision). That is a resolution failure in THIS gate's inputs, not\n        # evidence about the binding, so it must not be reported as \"your binding is invalid\".\n        rec.update(ok=False, code=\"FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED\", reader_code=reader_code,\n                   detail=(f\"the binding reader at {reader} produced no usable verdict (exit \"\n                           f\"{proc.returncode}, no recognised refusal code); a reader that cannot \"\n                           f\"decide cannot authorize a spawn: \"\n                           f\"{(proc.stderr or proc.stdout or '').strip()[-600:]}\"),\n                   stderr=(proc.stderr or \"\")[:2000])\n        return rec\n    rec.update(ok=False, code=guard_code, reader_code=reader_code,\n               detail=(f\"the binding reader refused this binding: exit {proc.returncode}, \"\n                       f\"reader code {reader_code!r} -> {guard_code}\"),\n               stderr=(proc.stderr or \"\")[:2000])\n    return rec\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# binding record access (field LOCATION tolerance only; no predicate here)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef binding_arm(rec: dict) -> str | None:\n    provider, model = rec.get(\"provider\"), rec.get(\"model\")\n    if isinstance(provider, str) and isinstance(model, str) and provider and model:\n        return f\"{provider}/{model}\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef binding_field(rec: dict, name: str):\n    \"\"\"Read `name` where the shipped binding revisions put it (top level, or nested under `epoch`).\"\"\"\n    if name in rec:\n        return rec[name]\n    nested = rec.get(\"epoch\")\n    if isinstance(nested, dict) and name in nested:\n        return nested[name]\n    return None\n\n\ndef binding_id_of(rec: dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The record's own binding_id when it declares one, else the digest of its body.\"\"\"\n    declared = rec.get(\"binding_id\")\n    if isinstance(declared, str) and len(declared) == 64:\n        return declared\n    body = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != \"binding_id\"}\n    return canonical_digest(body)\n\n\ndef arm_retirement(arm: str, registry: dict, pool: dict) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"The retirement ledger entry for `arm`, if ANY ledger declares it (registry or pool).\n\n    Both ledgers are merged FIELD BY FIELD (registry first, then pool filling gaps): the registry scan\n    records the arm names of its `retired_arms:` block without parsing the nested prose, so a field\n    the registry copy omits is taken from the pool copy rather than reported as absent.\n    \"\"\"\n    hits = []\n    for name, ledger in ((\"registry\", registry.get(\"retired\")), (\"pool\", pool.get(\"retired\"))):\n        if isinstance(ledger, dict) and arm in ledger:\n            hits.append({\"ledger\": name, \"entry\": ledger[arm] if isinstance(ledger[arm], dict) else {}})\n    if not hits:\n        return None\n    merged: dict = {}\n    for hit in hits:\n        for key, value in (hit[\"entry\"] or {}).items():\n            if merged.get(key) in (None, \"\", {}) and value not in (None, \"\"):\n                merged[key] = value\n    return {\"arm\": arm, \"ledgers\": hits, \"replacement\": merged.get(\"replacement\"),\n            \"reason\": merged.get(\"reason\"), \"retired_epoch\": merged.get(\"retired_epoch\"),\n            \"ordered_by\": merged.get(\"ordered_by\")}\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# the gate\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef gate_entry(binding=None, lane: str = \"\", arm=None, hgk_root=None, reader=None,\n               require_bound: bool = False, now: datetime.datetime | None = None) -> tuple[int, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Re-verify the binding for `lane` and decide whether `arm` may enter. Returns (exit, record).\n\n    `exit == 0` is the ONLY value on which a caller may create a child. Every other value carries a\n    named code from EXIT_CODES plus the check list that produced it.\n    \"\"\"\n    now = now or utc_now()\n    rec: dict = {\"schema\": SCHEMA, \"ok\": False, \"code\": None, \"detail\": None,\n                 \"generated_utc\": iso(now), \"lane\": lane, \"arm\": arm, \"checks\": []}\n\n    def fail(code: str, detail: str, **extra) -> tuple[int, dict]:\n        rec.update(ok=False, code=code, detail=detail, **extra)\n        rec[\"check\"] = code\n        return EXIT_CODES[code], rec\n\n    def note(cid: str, ok: bool, **extra) -> None:\n        rec[\"checks\"].append({\"id\": cid, \"ok\": ok, **extra})\n\n    # 1. the binding must exist ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    if not binding:\n        note(\"binding_present\", False, reason=\"no --binding supplied\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_ASSERT_REQUIRED\",\n                    \"no --binding was supplied; a fresh process has no session binding, and an \"\n                    \"unbound run must not reach a spawn\")\n    bpath = pathlib.Path(binding)\n    if not bpath.is_file():\n        note(\"binding_present\", False, reason=f\"absent: {bpath}\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_ASSERT_REQUIRED\", f\"the binding file is absent: {bpath}\")\n    brec, berr = read_json(bpath)\n    if brec is None:\n        note(\"binding_parse\", False, reason=berr)\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\", f\"{bpath}: {berr}\")\n    rec[\"binding_path\"] = str(bpath)\n    rec[\"binding_sha256\"] = sha256_file(bpath)\n    rec[\"binding_id\"] = binding_id_of(brec)\n    rec[\"binding_run_id\"] = binding_field(brec, \"run_id\")\n    rec[\"binding_session_id\"] = binding_field(brec, \"session_id\")\n    note(\"binding_parse\", True, binding_id=rec[\"binding_id\"], run_id=rec.get(\"binding_run_id\"))\n\n    # 2. the control plane (registry + pool) must resolve -------------------------------------\n    root, root_source = resolve_hgk_root(hgk_root)\n    if root is None:\n        note(\"hgk_root\", False, reason=\"unresolved\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED\",\n                    \"the HGK control plane could not be resolved (no --hgk-root, no $HGK_ROOT, no \"\n                    \"config/hgk-root.txt, and this repo carries no config/lane-routing-registry.yaml)\")\n    registry = scan_registry(root / \"config\" / \"lane-routing-registry.yaml\")\n    pool = scan_pool(root / \"config\" / \"lane-route-slots.candidate.json\")\n    rec.update(hgk_root=str(root), hgk_root_source=root_source,\n               registry={k: registry[k] for k in (\"path\", \"sha256\", \"epoch\", \"status\",\n                                                  \"contract_epoch\", \"error\")},\n               pool={k: pool[k] for k in (\"path\", \"sha256\", \"epoch\", \"error\")})\n    if not registry[\"sha256\"] or not pool[\"sha256\"]:\n        note(\"control_plane_readable\", False, registry=registry[\"error\"], pool=pool[\"error\"])\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED\",\n                    f\"the control plane at {root} is unreadable: registry={registry['error']!r} \"\n                    f\"pool={pool['error']!r}\")\n    note(\"control_plane_readable\", True, epoch=registry[\"epoch\"], pool_epoch=pool[\"epoch\"])\n\n    # 3. arm under test: the caller's, else the lane's rank-1 pool arm ------------------------\n    arm_source = \"flag:--arm\"\n    if not arm:\n        rank1 = next((r for r in pool[\"lanes\"].get(lane, []) if r[\"dispatchable\"]), None)\n        arm = rank1[\"arm\"] if rank1 else None\n        arm_source = \"derived:pool-rank-1\"\n    rec[\"arm_source\"] = arm_source\n    if not arm:\n        note(\"arm_resolved\", False, reason=f\"no arm for lane {lane}\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_ARM_MISMATCH\",\n                    f\"no arm was named and lane {lane!r} has no dispatchable pool arm at {pool['path']}\")\n    rec[\"arm\"] = arm\n    note(\"arm_resolved\", True, arm=arm, arm_source=arm_source)\n\n    # 4. epoch drift: registry bytes (and numeric epoch) the binding was minted against --------\n    bound_registry_sha = binding_field(brec, \"registry_sha256\")\n    if not bound_registry_sha:\n        note(\"registry_digest_present\", False, reason=\"the binding records no registry_sha256\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\",\n                    f\"the binding {bpath} records no registry_sha256, so no epoch can be verified; \"\n                    f\"a binding that cannot name its epoch is not evidence\")\n    if bound_registry_sha != registry[\"sha256\"]:\n        note(\"registry_digest\", False, bound=bound_registry_sha, now=registry[\"sha256\"])\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_EPOCH_DRIFT\",\n                    f\"the registry bytes moved since the binding was minted: bound \"\n                    f\"{bound_registry_sha[:16]}..., now {registry['sha256'][:16]}... \"\n                    f\"({registry['path']})\")\n    bound_epoch = binding_field(brec, \"policy_epoch\")\n    if bound_epoch is None:\n        bound_epoch = binding_field(brec, \"epoch\")\n    # a binding that nests its epoch record under `epoch` also names its numeric epoch there\n    if isinstance(bound_epoch, dict):\n        bound_epoch = bound_epoch.get(\"policy_epoch\")\n    if bound_epoch is not None and registry[\"epoch\"] is not None and bound_epoch != registry[\"epoch\"]:\n        note(\"registry_epoch\", False, bound=bound_epoch, now=registry[\"epoch\"])\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_EPOCH_DRIFT\",\n                    f\"the binding was minted at epoch {bound_epoch!r} but the registry declares \"\n                    f\"{registry['epoch']!r}\")\n    note(\"registry_digest\", True, sha256=registry[\"sha256\"], epoch=registry[\"epoch\"])\n\n    # 5. pool drift: the pool bytes the binding was minted against -----------------------------\n    bound_pool_sha = binding_field(brec, \"pool_sha256\")\n    if not bound_pool_sha:\n        note(\"pool_digest_present\", False, reason=\"the binding records no pool_sha256\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\",\n                    f\"the binding {bpath} records no pool_sha256, so the pool it was admitted \"\n                    f\"against cannot be identified\")\n    if bound_pool_sha != pool[\"sha256\"]:\n        note(\"pool_digest\", False, bound=bound_pool_sha, now=pool[\"sha256\"])\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_POOL_DRIFT\",\n                    f\"the pool bytes moved since the binding was minted: bound \"\n                    f\"{bound_pool_sha[:16]}..., now {pool['sha256'][:16]}... ({pool['path']})\")\n    note(\"pool_digest\", True, sha256=pool[\"sha256\"])\n\n    # 6. the requested arm must not be retired (registry ledger AND pool ledger) --------------\n    retired = arm_retirement(arm, registry, pool)\n    if retired:\n        note(\"retirement_ledger\", False, **retired)\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_RETIRED_ARM\",\n                    f\"the arm {arm} is RETIRED ({retired['reason'] or 'no reason recorded'}); \"\n                    f\"replacement is {retired['replacement']!r}; a retired arm is never selectable\",\n                    retirement=retired)\n    note(\"retirement_ledger\", True, arm=arm)\n\n    # 7. expiry -------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    expires_at = parse_iso(binding_field(brec, \"expires_at\"))\n    if expires_at is not None and expires_at <= now:\n        note(\"binding_expiry\", False, expires_at=iso(expires_at), now=iso(now))\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_EXPIRED\",\n                    f\"the binding expired at {iso(expires_at)} (now {iso(now)}); a binding is a \"\n                    f\"time-bounded fact, not a standing permission\")\n    note(\"binding_expiry\", True, expires_at=iso(expires_at) if expires_at else None)\n\n    # 8. the arm actually being entered must be the bound arm ---------------------------------\n    bound_arm = binding_arm(brec)\n    if bound_arm is None:\n        note(\"bound_arm_present\", False, reason=\"the binding names no provider/model\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\",\n                    f\"the binding {bpath} names no provider/model, so no arm can be matched\")\n    if bound_arm.strip().lower() != str(arm).strip().lower():\n        note(\"arm_match\", False, bound=bound_arm, requested=arm)\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_ARM_MISMATCH\",\n                    f\"the binding authorizes {bound_arm} but this entry asks for {arm}; the token \"\n                    f\"must name the arm that will be spawned\")\n    note(\"arm_match\", True, arm=bound_arm)\n\n    # 9. --require-bound: the record must carry what makes it a HARD-entry binding ------------\n    if require_bound:\n        missing = [f for f in (\"run_id\", \"session_id\") if not binding_field(brec, f)]\n        if not isinstance(brec.get(\"binding_id\"), str):\n            missing.append(\"binding_id\")\n        if missing:\n            note(\"require_bound\", False, missing=missing)\n            return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\",\n                        f\"--require-bound is set and the binding omits {missing}; a lane-wide \"\n                        f\"default is not a run-scoped binding\")\n        note(\"require_bound\", True, run_id=binding_field(brec, \"run_id\"))\n    else:\n        note(\"require_bound\", True, enforced=False)\n\n    # 10. the single-source predicate: the SBL binding reader, by PATH ------------------------\n    reader_path, reader_source = resolve_reader(reader)\n    rec[\"reader_source\"] = reader_source\n    if reader_path is None:\n        note(\"reader_resolved\", False, reason=\"unresolved\")\n        return fail(\"FHS_ENTRY_GUARD_UNRESOLVED\",\n                    \"--binding-reader was not supplied and no discovery file / $FHS_3LM_PATH \"\n                    \"names the SBL reader; this gate will not re-implement the binding predicate\")\n    verdict = call_reader(reader_path, bpath, root)\n    rec[\"reader\"] = verdict\n    note(\"reader_resolved\", True, path=str(reader_path), source=reader_source)\n    if not verdict.get(\"ok\"):\n        return fail(verdict.get(\"code\") or \"FHS_ENTRY_BINDING_INVALID\",\n                    verdict.get(\"detail\") or \"the binding reader refused this binding\",\n                    reader=verdict)\n    note(\"reader_assert\", True, exit=verdict.get(\"exit\"))\n\n    rec.update(ok=True, code=None, detail=None, check=\"PASS\")\n    return 0, rec\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# one-shot run-scoped capability\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef capability_path(entry_dir, run_id: str) -> pathlib.Path:\n    return pathlib.Path(entry_dir or DEFAULT_ENTRY_DIR) / f\"{run_id}.json\"\n\n\ndef spent_marker(entry_dir, run_id: str) -> pathlib.Path:\n    return pathlib.Path(entry_dir or DEFAULT_ENTRY_DIR) / f\"{run_id}.spent\"\n\n\ndef mint_capability(run_id: str, token: str, lane: str, arm: str, gate: dict,\n                    entry_dir=None, ttl: int = DEFAULT_TTL_SECONDS,\n                    now: datetime.datetime | None = None) -> tuple[int, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Write `var/fhs-entry/<run_id>.json` with O_CREAT|O_EXCL. One run_id, one capability.\"\"\"\n    now = now or utc_now()\n    if not run_id:\n        return 28, {\"schema\": CAPABILITY_SCHEMA, \"ok\": False, \"code\": \"FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_REQUIRED\",\n                    \"detail\": \"--guard-token needs --run-id: the capability is keyed by the run it \"\n                              \"authorizes, and a token nobody can look up authorizes nothing\"}\n    path = capability_path(entry_dir, run_id)\n    if path.exists():\n        return 29, {\"schema\": CAPABILITY_SCHEMA, \"ok\": False, \"code\": \"FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_CONSUMED\",\n                    \"detail\": f\"a capability already exists for run_id {run_id!r} at {path}; a \"\n                              f\"second mint would be a replay of a spent run scope\",\n                    \"path\": str(path)}\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    body = {\n        \"schema\": CAPABILITY_SCHEMA,\n        \"run_id\": run_id, \"token\": token,\n        \"lane\": lane, \"arm\": arm,\n        \"binding_path\": gate.get(\"binding_path\"), \"binding_id\": gate.get(\"binding_id\"),\n        \"binding_sha256\": gate.get(\"binding_sha256\"),\n        \"registry_sha256\": (gate.get(\"registry\") or {}).get(\"sha256\"),\n        \"pool_sha256\": (gate.get(\"pool\") or {}).get(\"sha256\"),\n        \"minted_by_pid\": os.getpid(), \"minted_at\": iso(now),\n        \"expires_at\": iso(now + datetime.timedelta(seconds=int(ttl))),\n        \"ttl_seconds\": int(ttl), \"spent\": False,\n        \"scope\": \"one dispatch run; consumed by scripts/hgk-lane-dispatch.py before any child\",\n    }\n    body[\"capability_id\"] = canonical_digest(body)\n    payload = (json.dumps(body, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True) + \"\\n\").encode(\"utf-8\")\n    try:\n        fd = os.open(str(path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)\n    except FileExistsError:\n        return 29, {\"schema\": CAPABILITY_SCHEMA, \"ok\": False, \"code\": \"FHS_ENTRY_TOKEN_CONSUMED\",\n                    \"detail\": f\"the capability for run_id {run_id!r} appeared while minting \"\n                              f\"(O_EXCL); This tool removes the field as the\r\nsource of truth: a grant is now valid because an Ed25519 signature over its own payload verifies against a key id,\r\nthe nonce has never been spent, and the TTL has not expired. `assurance_level` is derived from *which key signed it*,\r\nnever typed in.\r\n\r\nKey handling, stated plainly:\r\n\r\n* The private key lives at `config/issuer/issuer.key`, 32 raw seed bytes, and is protected by **DPAPI** when the\r\n  platform allows it (Windows encrypts it to the owner's own logon — no passphrase to type, nothing in the chat, and\r\n  the file is useless if copied to another account). Where DPAPI is unavailable it stays a plain seed file and the\r\n  receipt says so.\r\n* `key_id = sha256(public_key)[:16]`. Rotation is a new file, never an edited one.\r\n* Every issued grant consumes a nonce written to `config/issuer/nonce-ledger.jsonl`. A replayed nonce fails.\r\n\r\nThe owner authorization that this key acts under is recorded verbatim in `config/issuer/OWNER_AUTHORIZATION.json`\r\nand its sha is bound into every grant, so a grant can always be traced to the sentence that permitted it.\r\n\r\n  issue_grant.py init\r\n  issue_grant.py issue --principal P --operation install_occupant --writeset WS.json [--ttl 900]\r\n  issue_grant.py verify --grant G.json\r\n  issue_grant.py public-key\r\n\"\"\"\r\n\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\n\r\ntry:  # every child of this process must be consoleless; see      return ctypes.string_at(bl.pbData, bl.cbData)\r\n\r\n        crypt32 = ctypes.windll.crypt32\r\n        fn = crypt32.CryptProtectData if protect else crypt32.CryptUnprotectData\r\n        src, It holds a *capability token*: an owner-signed, scope-bounded,\r\nexpiring delegation that an Issuer can verify against the owner's PUBLIC key. That is the difference\r\nbetween \"the agent can sign anything\" (rejected) and \"the owner decided once, in writing, what may run\r\nunattended\" (this).\r\n\r\nWHAT IT REFUSES TO DO\r\n  * print, This tool removes the field as the\r\nsource of truth: a grant is now valid because an Ed25519 signature over its own payload verifies against a key id,\r\nthe nonce has never been spent, and the TTL has not expired. `assurance_level` is derived from *which key signed it*,\r\nnever typed in.\r\n\r\nKey handling, stated plainly:\r\n\r\n* The private key lives at `config/issuer/issuer.key`, 32 raw seed bytes, and is protected by **DPAPI** when the\r\n  platform allows it (Windows encrypts it to the owner's own logon — no passphrase to type, nothing in the chat, and\r\n  the file is useless if copied to another account). Where DPAPI is unavailable it stays a plain seed file and the\r\n  receipt says so.\r\n* `key_id = sha256(public_key)[:16]`. Rotation is a new file, never an edited one.\r\n* Every issued grant consumes a nonce written to `config/issuer/nonce-ledger.jsonl`. A replayed nonce fails.\r\n\r\nThe owner authorization that this key acts under is recorded verbatim in `config/issuer/OWNER_AUTHORIZATION.json`\r\nand its sha is bound into every grant, so a grant 錯層稽核\n\n| 層 | 位置 | 本回合放進去什麼 | 判定 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 權威層 | `SBL/main`、`Fabric/main` | 無（合併在驗收 PASS 之後才做） | 正確——未提權 |\n| 候選層 | PR #58（`docs/fhs-w0-r17-reader-acks`）、PR #44 | r20 全部交付物與證據 | 正確 |\n| 作者工作層 | `FHS_W0_R13_ACCEPTANCE_20261108/`（writeback、tools、lanes、receipts、out） | 全部來源 | 正確 |\n| 可棄層 | `out/stage/**`、`%TEMP%/fhs_gate_mut_*` | 由作者層生成的載荷副本／自測副本 | 正確（可重建、回合結束刪除） |\n\n**控制平面變更：無。** 本回合沒有修改 `HG-KSEOS` 任一檔案。車道派送所需的 codex 執行檔以\n`--codex-executable` 逐次指定，config 內的 pin 保持原樣（見 §6）。\n\n## 2. 兩個缺陷的處置\n\n### CE-07 `r19-inplace-mutation`\n\n- 病因：`gate_r19.py` 的 `MUTATIONS` 把突變 bytes 寫進回合目錄的檔案再還原（`gate_r19.py:1520-1549`）。\n  在 `--sandbox read-only` 下第一寫入即被拒，於是「突變會 FIRED」與「已還原」只有具寫入權的作者能主張。\n- 修法：`gate_r20.py` 的 `selftest()` 先 `mirror_round()` 複製到 `%TEMP%`，所有 baseline 與 mutated\n  執行都讀副本；結束時比對 `tree_fingerprint()` 前後值，相等才輸出 `round_tree_unchanged: true`。\n- 證據：`out/GATE_R20_SELFTEST.json` 的 `wrote_inside_round`、`round_tree_unchanged`、\n  `round_tree_fingerprint_before/after`。\n- 自傷紀錄：第一次修法用 `FHS_G27_TEXT` 環境變數傳 621 KB 文件，撞上 Windows 32,767 字元上限，\n  自測回報 `G27 ERROR:ValueError`。改成模組層級 `_G27_TEXT_OVERRIDE` 後才 FIRED。\n\n### CE-08 `r19-label-scope`\n\n- 病因：`label_discipline.py` v1 只掃 markdown 與 JSON 的 `label|kind|claim` 三個鍵（大小寫敏感），\n  於是 `body.kind = \"original_reader_ack\"` 與 `consequence` 散文欄位完全在視野之外。\n- 修法：v2 走訪 `out/*.json`、`receipts/**/*.json`、`evidence/*.json` 的每一個字串值；合格前綴為\n  `FHS_`、`BARE_`，或是一條明示箭頭的改名紀錄（`裸 token -> fhs_ token`）；`not_claimed` /\n  `*_verbatim` 鍵下的字串屬否定容器或逐字引述，不算宣稱。掃描面寫在 `out/SCAN_SCOPE.json`，\n  每個排除都附理由；試圖排除 `receipts/`、`out/PRINCIPAL_DEFINITION.json`、\n  `out/FHS_ORIGINAL_ACK.json` 的 scope 會被拒絕（`SCOPE_EXCLUDES_A_MUST_SCAN_ARTEFACT`）。\n- 證據：`out/LABEL_DISCIPLINE_R20.json`（`machine_count: 0`）、`tools/gate_r20.py` 的 `G28`。\n\n## 3. 閘 v6（21 項）\n\n`G10,G11,G12,G13,G14,G15,G16a,G16b,G16c,G17,G18,G19,G20,G21,G22,G23,G24,G25,G26,G27,G28`\n\n新增 `G28 MACHINE_READABLE_QUALIFIER`（BLOCKING）。`G16b` 寫進回合目錄的檔案再還原（`gate_r19.py:1520-1549`）。\n  在 `--sandbox read-only` 下第一寫入即被拒，於是「突變會 FIRED」與「已還原」只有具寫入權的作者能主張。\n- 修法：`gate_r20.py` 的 `selftest()` 先 `mirror_round()` 複製到 `%TEMP%`，所有 baseline 與 mutated\n  執行都讀副本；結束時比對 `tree_fingerprint()` 前後值，相等才輸出 `round_tree_unchanged: true`。\n- 證據：`out/GATE_R20_SELFTEST.json` 的 `wrote_inside_round`、`round_tree_unchanged`、\n  `round_tree_fingerprint_before/after`。\n- 自傷紀錄：第一次修法用 `FHS_G27_TEXT` 環境變數傳 621 KB 文件，撞上 Windows 32,767 字元上限，\n  自測回報 `G27 ERROR:ValueError`。改成模組層級 `_G27_TEXT_OVERRIDE` 後才 FIRED。\n\n### CE-08 `r19-label-scope`\n\n- 病因：`label_discipline.py` v1 只掃 markdown 與 JSON 的 `label|kind|claim` 三個鍵（大小寫敏感），\n  於是 `body.kind = \"original_reader_ack\"` 與 `consequence` 散文欄位完全在視野之外。\n- 修法：v2 走訪 `out/*.json`、`receipts/**/*.json`、`evidence/*.json` 的每一個字串值；合格前綴為\n  `FHS_`、`BARE_`，或是一條明示箭頭的改名紀錄（`裸 token -> fhs_ token`）；`not_claimed` /\n  `*_verbatim` 鍵下的字串屬否定容器或逐字引述，不算宣稱。掃描面寫在 `out/SCAN_SCOPE.json`，\n  每個排除都附理由；試圖排除 `receipts/`、`out/PRINCIPAL_DEFINITION.json`、\n  `out/FHS_ORIGINAL_ACK.json` 的 scope 會被拒絕（`SCOPE_EXCLUDES_A_MUST_SCAN_ARTEFACT`）。\n- 證據：`out/LABEL_DISCIPLINE_R20.json`（`machine_count: 0`）、`tools/gate_r20.py` 的 `G28`。\n\n## 3. 閘 v6（21 項）\n\n`G10,G11,G12,G13,G14,G15,G16a,G16b,G16c,G17,G18,G19,G20,G21,G22,G23,G24,G25,G26,G27,G28`\n\n新增 `G28 MACHINE_READABLE_QUALIFIER`（BLOCKING）。`G16b` 維持 `REPORTING`／`OPEN`：\n`hgk-main` 在本機只落地釘住 commit 的三個檔案，因此「預設安裝是否覆蓋 import」只能宣告為\n未完成掃描。這一項不是 PASS，也不計入 blocking。\n\n自測退出碼約定：全部突變 FIRED 且回合樹指紋未變 → exit 0；任一列未 FIRED 或指紋改變 → exit 1。\n`python tools/gate_r20.py --round . --skip-hygiene --selftest`   # exit 0\n\n## 4. 明確不主張\n\n- 傳統 per-domain `ORIGINAL_ACK`（實測 0/6）。\n- OS 帳號層級或人身層級的身分分離；三車道在同一 Windows 帳號下。\n- `G16b` 已閉合。\n- 已合併、已審、已發行——除非 §5
